Notes:

- Leading Renoise separator marker `--` is stripped for grouping and shown as `[separator]` on the source line.
- The Exact Static Tree contains only paths whose menu text is fully known from source.
- Dynamic pieces that cannot be known statically are shown as `{identifier}` or `{unresolved: ...}` in the separate dynamic section.
- Commented-out menu registrations are skipped.
